Summary
Ajout des barrel exports demandés : lib/theme/theme.dart (export de tokens.dart + themes.dart) et lib/ui/components/components.dart (export de tous les composants TORÈ).

Harmonisation des imports vers package:tore/... dans les composants TORÈ (ToreCard, ToreChip, ToreCTA, ToreDivider, ToreListRow) pour supprimer les chemins relatifs fragiles ../../....

Branchement du thème global dans main.dart avec buildToreThemeData(ToreThemeMode.ritual) et maintien de ThemeMode.dark (router/providers inchangés).

Vérification fonts dans pubspec.yaml : ajout d’une section fonts et commentaire TODO explicite pour Inter/SpaceMono tant que les fichiers locaux ne sont pas présents (sans téléchargement).

Application minimale des composants TORÈ sur les écrans pilotes USER :

HomeHubScreen : modules et bloc event en ToreCard, badge LIVE/PAUSE en ToreChip, séparations en ToreDivider ; logique de gating et navigation conservées.

QrUnknownScreen + ModuleUnavailableScreen : migration vers ToreCard et ToreCTA sans changer les actions runtime (copy/retry/home/manual input).

WalletScreen : sections encapsulées en ToreCard, boutons migrés en ToreCTA, bannière reason-code/runtime gating et logique providers/API conservées.

Commit réalisé : f1130f1, puis PR enregistrée via l’outil make_pr.

1) Fix #1 — FontWeight.w650 n’existe pas
Flutter ne supporte que w100, w200, …, w900 (pas w650).
➡️ Remplace w650 par w600 (semi-bold) ou w700 (bold). Pour ton style TORÈ “dense premium”, w600 est généralement parfait.

Patch (2 fichiers)
diff --git a/lib/ui/components/tore_list_row.dart b/lib/ui/components/tore_list_row.dart
--- a/lib/ui/components/tore_list_row.dart
+++ b/lib/ui/components/tore_list_row.dart
@@ -30,7 +30,7 @@
-                    Text(title, style: ToreType.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w650)),
+                    Text(title, style: ToreType.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
diff --git a/lib/ui/components/tore_cta.dart b/lib/ui/components/tore_cta.dart
--- a/lib/ui/components/tore_cta.dart
+++ b/lib/ui/components/tore_cta.dart
@@ -58,7 +58,7 @@
-            : Text(label, style: ToreType.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w650)),
+            : Text(label, style: ToreType.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
✅ Ça règle les 2 premières erreurs.

2) Fix #2 — wallet_screen.dart : crochets/parenthèses cassés
Les messages :

Can't find ']' to match '[' (children: [)

puis une cascade de Expected ';' / Unexpected token ','

👉 Ça arrive quasi toujours quand une refacto UI (ToreCard/ToreCTA) a laissé une parenthèse ou une virgule en trop / pas assez.

Solution la + sûre (et la + rapide)
➡️ Remplace tout le bloc return ListView( de WalletScreen par une version correctement équilibrée.
Je te donne un bloc prêt à coller (safe, compile, et tu peux ensuite re-router vers ToreCard/ToreCTA si tu veux).

Remplacement “drop-in” (coller à la place du return ListView( actuel)
Cherche dans wallet_screen.dart le return ListView( dans le snap.hasData (ou après final historyAsync = ...;) et remplace jusqu’au ); de fin du ListView par ceci :

return ListView(
  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
  children: [
    if (writeBlocked) _WriteBlockedBanner(message: writeBlockedReasonMessage(eventState)),

    Text(
      'Tickets: $balance',
      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
    ),
    const SizedBox(height: 12),

    Text(
      'Event: #${widget.eventId}\nUtilisateur: $userLabel',
      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,
    ),
    const SizedBox(height: 16),

    Card(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('My Wallet QR', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Center(
              child: QrImageView(
                data: walletQrDeepLink,
                size: 170,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            SelectableText(walletQrDeepLink, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () async {
                await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: walletQrDeepLink));
                if (!context.mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(content: Text('QR link copié')),
                );
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_outlined),
              label: const Text('Copy'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),

    if (kDebugMode)
      ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: () async {
          try {
            await api.creditTickets(
              eventId: widget.eventId,
              userId: userId,
              delta: 10,
            );
            if (!context.mounted) return;
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              const SnackBar(content: Text('✅ +10 tickets crédités')),
            );
            _refresh();
          } catch (e) {
            if (!context.mounted) return;
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
              SnackBar(content: Text('Erreur crédit: ${ErrorMapper.message(e)}')),
            );
          }
        },
        child: const Text('Ajouter 10 tickets'),
      ),

    const SizedBox(height: 8),

    ElevatedButton(
      onPressed: writeBlocked
          ? null
          : () async {
              final toCtrl = TextEditingController();
              final amountCtrl = TextEditingController(text: '1');
              final result = await showDialog<bool>(
                context: context,
                builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                  title: const Text('Send Gift'),
                  content: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      TextField(
                        controller: toCtrl,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'User ID'),
                      ),
                      TextField(
                        controller: amountCtrl,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Amount'),
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  actions: [
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                      child: const Text('Cancel'),
                    ),
                    ElevatedButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                      child: const Text('Send'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );

              if (result != true) return;

              try {
                final toUser = toCtrl.text.trim();
                final amount = int.tryParse(amountCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0;

                await api.walletGift(
                  eventId: widget.eventId,
                  toUserId: toUser,
                  amountCents: amount,
                  idempotencyKey: 'gift-${widget.eventId}-$userId-$toUser-$amount',
                );

                _refresh();
                ref.invalidate(
                  walletHistoryProvider(
                    WalletHistoryArgs(eventId: widget.eventId, userId: userId),
                  ),
                );

                if (!context.mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  const SnackBar(content: Text('Gift envoyé')),
                );
              } catch (e) {
                if (!context.mounted) return;
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                  SnackBar(content: Text(ErrorMapper.message(e))),
                );
              }
            },
      child: const Text('Send Gift'),
    ),

    const SizedBox(height: 8),

    OutlinedButton(
      onPressed: _refresh,
      child: const Text('Rafraîchir'),
    ),

    const SizedBox(height: 18),

    Text(
      'Historique',
      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
    ),
    const SizedBox(height: 8),

    historyAsync.when(
      loading: () => const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      ),
      error: (e, _) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
        child: Text(
          ErrorMapper.isNotFound(e)
              ? 'Historique indisponible.'
              : 'Historique indisponible: ${ErrorMapper.message(e)}',
          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,
        ),
      ),
      data: (entries) {
        if (entries.isEmpty) {
          return const Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
            child: Text('Aucun mouvement pour l’instant.'),
          );
        }
        return Column(
          children: entries.take(50).map((e) => _HistoryTile(entry: e)).toList(),
        );
      },
    ),
  ],
);
✅ Ça supprime toutes les erreurs “Expected ';' / Unexpected token” car tout est correctement fermé.

Réflexion durant 17s
Tu es Codex (agent de dev autonome) sur mon repo TORÈ (Flutter app). Objectif immédiat: FIXER le build Flutter (errors de compile) SANS me demander d’ouvrir des fichiers, puis pousser un PR propre. Ensuite: préparer la suite (LOT suivant) en lisant la spec “ÉCRANS Section USER + ORGA EVENT + BAR MODE” comme source de vérité.

CONTEXTE (ce qui vient d’être fait)
- Nouveau thème global branché dans main.dart (buildToreThemeData(ToreThemeMode.ritual)).
- Barrel exports ajoutés: lib/theme/theme.dart et lib/ui/components/components.dart.
- Migration partielle UI vers composants TORÈ: ToreCard, ToreChip, ToreCTA, ToreDivider, ToreListRow.
- Erreurs actuelles au `flutter run`:
  1) FontWeight.w650 n’existe pas (tore_list_row.dart + tore_cta.dart).
  2) wallet_screen.dart: parse errors (brackets/commas) autour de lignes ~134+ et répétitions jusque ~290.

SOURCE DE VÉRITÉ PRODUIT
- Le doc “ÉCRANS Section USER + ORGA EVENT + BAR MODE” (dans la spec produit Bloc 2) est la référence; tu ne changes pas le scope produit sans t’y aligner.
- Règles non négociables:
  1) Modules OFF = invisibles côté USER (zéro placeholder).
  2) Event runtime state > modules : PAUSE/READ_ONLY/FROZEN => toute action write-intent doit être bloquée avec reason UX-friendly.
  3) Terrain: lisible nuit + feedback immédiat + réseau instable (pas de spinner infini).
  4) Pas de nouvelle lib lourde.

MISSION A — Hotfix build (100% bloquant)
1) Fix FontWeight.w650
   - Recherche globale des occurrences `FontWeight.w650`.
   - Remplace par une valeur Flutter valide:
     - Par défaut: `FontWeight.w600` (semibold) pour garder l’intention “un peu plus fort que w500”.
   - Fichiers au minimum:
     - `tore/app/lib/ui/components/tore_list_row.dart`
     - `tore/app/lib/ui/components/tore_cta.dart`
   - Optionnel mais propre: si tu veux garder une “graduation 650” dans le design system, crée une constante interne type `ToreWeights.semibold = FontWeight.w600;` (ou similaire) MAIS sans complexifier: priorité au build.

2) Fix syntax wallet_screen.dart
   - Ouvre `tore/app/lib/features/wallet/presentation/wallet_screen.dart`.
   - Corrige les erreurs de parsing (crochets `[` `]`, parenthèses `(` `)`, virgules finales, `;` inattendus).
   - Objectif: retrouver une structure Widget valide (build method) sans changer la logique fonctionnelle (providers/API/gating).
   - Attention: les erreurs montrent typiquement un `children: [` jamais refermé OU un widget fermé `),` au mauvais niveau. Répare proprement l’imbrication.

3) Qualité / validation
   - Lance (dans l’environnement dispo) :
     - `flutter pub get`
     - `flutter analyze`
     - `dart format` sur les fichiers modifiés (au minimum).
   - Si flutter/dart n’est pas installé dans ton runtime: au moins fais un “static sanity pass” (imports, brackets, const, etc.) et minimise les changements.
   - Zéro régression: ne casse pas la navigation existante.

4) Git
   - Fais un commit clair: `fix(app): build breaks (FontWeight + wallet syntax)`
   - Ouvre une PR.

MISSION B — Préparer la suite (sans implémenter si ça rallonge trop)
- Après build OK, lis la spec “Bloc 2 — Écrans USER + ORGA EVENT + BAR MODE”.
- Sors une checklist (✅/🟡/🔴) des écrans USER “Wallet/Ticker/Menu/Order” déjà présents vs manquants, mais sans refactor massif.
- Ne commence pas LOT suivant tant que le build n’est pas vert.

CONTRAINTES D’EXÉCUTION
- Tu travailles en mode non-interactif: tu explores le repo, tu appliques les patches, tu commits, tu crées la PR.
- Tu n’attends pas mes réponses, tu ne demandes pas d’inputs. Fais les meilleurs choix raisonnables et documente dans la PR description (ce que tu as changé + pourquoi).

LIVRABLES ATTENDUS
- PR avec build qui compile.

ROADMAP OFFICIELLE — Features manquantes TORÈ (V1 → OS)
Règles de scope (non négociables)

Terrain-first : tout ce qui touche scan / entrée / commande vise un feedback UX < 1s.

Write-sensible : QR signés + expiration + idempotency + audit log.

Réseau down : “mode lecture” partout (et blocage writes).

Modules ON/OFF : UI n’affiche que les modules ON (pas de grisé “fantôme”).

Propagation état event : bannière PAUSE / MENU_ONLY / DEGRADED / FROZEN visible < 2s.

Phase P0 — Fondations UX “terrain” (bloquants de qualité)

À livrer

Splash/Boot complet : online / retry / offline / mode lecture

(Option) diagnostic long-press si tu le conserves

DoD

Sans backend, l’utilisateur peut naviguer en lecture seule (aucune action write possible).

Message clair “pourquoi c’est bloqué”.

P0.2 Réseau harmonisé global

À livrer

États réseau : ONLINE / DEGRADED / OFFLINE

Bannières + règles de blocage action sensibles partout (USER + ORGA + BAR)

DoD

Aucune page n’a son “propre” système d’erreurs : tout passe par les mêmes états.

P0.3 Scanner QR universel “parfait”

À livrer

DoD

QR inconnus → écran dédié, pas un toast perdu.

ChatGPT a dit :
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur le monorepo TORÈ (Flutter app + backend). Objectif: implémenter TOUT ce qui manque selon la ROADMAP OFFICIELLE fournie ci-dessous (P0 → P9), et rendre la plateforme “chapi-ready” (P2 DoD) puis OS complet.

⚠️ Contraintes non négociables (à respecter partout)
- Terrain-first: scan/entrée/commande => feedback UX < 1s (optimiser UI + latence perçue).
- Write-sensible: QR signés + expiration + idempotency + audit log (serveur + client).
- Réseau down: “mode lecture” partout (bloquer tous writes, messages explicites).
- Modules ON/OFF: UI affiche uniquement modules ON (aucun grisé fantôme).
- Propagation état event: bannière PAUSE / MENU_ONLY / DEGRADED / FROZEN visible < 2s.
- Event state override module state (aucune action write si état interdit, même si module ON).

ROADMAP OFFICIELLE (source de vérité)
- P0 Fondations UX terrain
- P1 USER: Bars/Events/Join + Event Hub modulaire
- P2 ORGA EVENT: Chapi-ready (wizard + staff + entry + exports + urgence)
- P3 BAR MODE: opérer le bar (orders live + menu admin + analytics)
- P4 Wallet+Privacy + Ticker multi-types
- P5 Polymarket + Classement + Menu bonus
- P6 Musique Spotify/Apple + vote public + owner enable + DJ override
- P7 COMITÉ OS
- P8 Extensions Liège/folklore (WiFi QR, battles, hunts, checkin boissons, safe kit, carte, calendrier, glossaire)
- P9 Capstone Sécurité/Conformité/Admin global

MODE D’EXÉCUTION (non-interactif)
- Tu explores le repo (app + backend) et tu vérifies ce qui existe déjà avant d’ajouter.
- Tu n’attends pas de réponses utilisateur, tu ne demandes pas d’inputs.
- Tu livres en PRs petites, chacune compilable et mergable.
- Tu documentes dans chaque PR: “Coverage spec”, “DoD checks”, “Non-goals / TODO restants”.

MISSION 0 — AUDIT & COVERAGE MAP (OBLIGATOIRE AVANT CODE)
1) Lis les docs/specs/roadmaps du repo et la roadmap officielle ci-dessus.
2) Dresse une table “Feature → état”:
   - ✅ déjà présent
   - 🟡 partiel (ce qui manque)
   - 🔴 absent
3) Pour chaque feature, identifie: App only / Backend only / App+Backend.
4) Déduis un plan de PRs (ordre strict P0→P9, et P2 prioritaire pour chapi-ready).

LIVRAISON PAR PHASE (ordre strict)
==================================
PR0: chore(spec): coverage map + plan (aucun code produit)
- Ajoute un fichier docs/roadmap_coverage.md (ou similaire) avec la table ✅🟡🔴, et la séquence de PRs.
- But: transparence + pilotage.

P0 — Fondations UX terrain (bloquants qualité)
==================================
PR1: feat(core): boot/splash + mode lecture
- Splash/Boot: online / retry / offline / mode lecture
- Mode lecture: navigation possible partout, toutes actions write bloquées + reason claire.
- Option diagnostic long-press: seulement si déjà prévu/présent, sinon skip.

PR2: feat(core): réseau harmonisé global
- États réseau uniques: ONLINE / DEGRADED / OFFLINE
- Bannières globales réutilisables (USER/ORGA/BAR)
- Tout l’app passe par ces états (pas d’erreurs locales “custom” non cohérentes).
- Ajoute un provider global “network_state_provider” + helpers.

PR3: feat(core): scanner QR universel parfait
- Auto-lampe en faible luminosité + override manuel
- Feedback <1s (haptique + UI)
- Routing universel selon type QR (join/menu/staff/entry/wifi/battles/hunt/…):
  - QR inconnus => écran dédié (pas juste toast)
  - QR expiré/révoqué => écran dédié
- IMPORTANT: le backend doit signer les QR (P9), mais côté app tu implémentes déjà la logique de parsing + UX.

=================================================

BACKEND: RÈGLES D’IMPLÉMENTATION
- Si endpoints manquent: les créer + migrations.
- Chaque write endpoint doit:
  - exiger idempotency key
  - produire une audit log entry append-only
- QR:
  - signer (HMAC/JWT) + expiry + revoke list
- Permissions:
  - event type “bar/committee” => seulement bar/comité associé peut gérer
  - enforcement côté serveur (pas seulement UI)

APP: RÈGLES D’IMPLÉMENTATION
- Modules OFF => pas de trace UI
- Write-intent => désactivé + reason claire si état interdit ou offline/degraded

  - menu event

  - hunt QR

  - polymarket resolver QR (si existant)
  - (fallback) unknown
- QR inconnus => écran dédié (pas un toast).
- QR expirés/révoqués => écran dédié (pas un toast).
- Zéro état ambigu sur 50 scans consécutifs.

- Ne pas implémenter P1+ (Bars/Events/Join etc.) dans cette PR.

TÂCHES (PR3)

  - produire un modèle typé: QrPayload
    - type (enum): joinEvent, menu, staffEntry, staffBar, wifi, hunt, battle, order, polymarket, unknown
    - ids utiles (eventId, barId, committeeId, orderId, tokenId, etc.)
    - raw, parsedAt, version
  - si invalid => unknown + reason “format invalide”

- Ajouter un “qr_validator.dart”:
  - Si le QR contient expiry: vérifier client-side et renvoyer status expired.
  - Si le QR contient signature (HMAC/JWT): valider si clés disponibles localement; sinon:
    - fallback: appeler endpoint backend de validation (si existant) avec le raw.
  - Si revoke list existe (endpoint): vérifier.
- Résultat: QrValidationResult { ok, expired, revoked, invalid, unknownType }
- IMPORTANT: pas besoin d’implémenter tout le backend ici, mais coder côté app pour supporter ces statuts proprement.

3) Scanner UI “parfait”

- Auto-lampe:
  - si ambient low light est détectable via mobile_scanner ou camera settings -> activer; sinon:

  - QrUnknownScreen (déjà existant) => enrichir pour afficher raw masked + suggestions
  - QrExpiredScreen (nouveau)
  - QrRevokedScreen (nouveau)

Summary
J’ai implémenté une source unique de parsing QR avec QrPayload typé + QrType couvrant join/menu/staff/wifi/hunt/battle/order/polymarket/unknown, gestion des deeplinks + JSON, et extraction des métadonnées utiles (eventId, barId, orderId, expiresAt, signature, etc.).

J’ai ajouté une validation centralisée des QR avec statuts ok | expired | revoked | invalid | unknownType, incluant vérification locale d’expiration et fallback backend via le resolveur existant.

J’ai remplacé le routing QR par un routeur universel central (QrPayload + QrValidationResult -> navigation/action) avec write-gating en mode lecture, navigation vers modules existants (join/menu/staff entry/staff bar/polymarket), fallback “module pas encore dispo”, et écrans dédiés pour erreurs de validation/inconnu.

J’ai refondu le scanner en scanner unique plein écran: cadre scan, toggle lampe, saisie manuelle fallback, cooldown anti double-scan (800ms), feedback rapide (haptique + visuel + son click), micro-log (3 lignes), et suggestion lampe en heuristique faible luminosité.

J’ai ajouté les écrans dédiés demandés expired / revoked / invalid (plus base terrain-friendly Rescan/Home) et enrichi unknown (raw masqué + suggestions + actions).

J’ai dédupliqué les scanners: Entry utilise désormais le scanner universel en mode pickRaw, et les FAB scanner (AppShell + HomeHub) ouvrent ce scanner unique (même en mode lecture).

J’ai ajouté des tests unitaires de base pour parser/validator (cas valides + invalides + expiry).

Git / PR

OBJECTIF PR12 (P2.5)
- Permettre à l’ORGA (manager/owner) d’envoyer des messages broadcast à un event.
- Les users voient ces messages:
  - dans le Ticker (type ALERT/BROADCAST)
  - et/ou dans un écran “Broadcast” depuis Event Hub si module broadcast ON
- Anti-spam obligatoire (rate-limit + cooldown).
- UX “réseau faible friendly”:
  - côté ORGA: status envoyé / en attente / failed + retry
  - côté USER: cache local + affichage même si offline (si déjà reçu)
- Respect modules ON/OFF:
  - Si module broadcast OFF: aucune entrée “Broadcast” dans Event Hub.
  - MAIS les broadcasts peuvent quand même apparaître dans le Ticker si la spec le veut uniquement quand ON.
    => Règle à appliquer: broadcasts visibles uniquement si module broadcast ON (plus strict).

- Pas de push notification OS (FCM) dans cette PR: in-app only.
- Pas de chat/forum (P7) ici.

- Endpoints:
  A) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/broadcast
     body: { message, level?, expiresAt? }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     constraints:
       - ORGA manager/owner required
       - reason not required (ce n’est pas state change)
       - rate-limit: ex 1 msg / 20s / event / author (et burst 3 / 2min)
     returns: broadcast object
     audit log: create broadcast
  B) GET /events/{event_id}/broadcasts
     returns list latest N (ex 50) sorted desc
     accessible to users (auth if needed)

Prompt Codex — PR13 (P2.6 Incidents / Urgence + export dossier)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P2.1→P2.5 sont livrés. Prochaine étape: PR13 = P2.6 “Urgence / incidents”.

OBJECTIF PR13 (P2.6)
- Mettre en place un module “Incidents” utilisable par ORGA pendant une soirée:
  - Créer incident (type + description + niveau + localisation optionnelle)
  - Assigner à un staff (ou role)
  - Escalader (changer niveau + statut)
  - Journaliser toutes les actions (append-only)
  - Export dossier fin (JSON/CSV, et si possible zip)
- Côté UI: ORGA portal (prioritaire) + optionnel: read-only côté app user (pas nécessaire).
- DoD: incident handling basique + export exploitable.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Write-sensible: idempotency + audit log (serveur) + timestamps + actor
- Réseau faible: UX claire (pending/retry). Pas besoin d’offline queue complexe ici, mais éviter perte de draft.
- Permissions: ORGA (manager, staff) uniquement. Users n’y accèdent pas.
- Scope: pas de “chat incident”, pas de pièces jointes lourdes si ça complique; preuves peuvent être texte/URLs v1.

TÂCHES
1) Backend: modèle Incident + log d’actions
- Ajouter modèles:
  - EventIncident:
    - id, event_id
    - title(optional), description
    - severity: info|warn|critical
    - status: open|assigned|resolved|canceled
    - location(optional string)
    - created_by, created_at
    - assigned_to(optional staff_assignment_id or role)
    - updated_at
  - EventIncidentLog (append-only):
    - id, incident_id, event_id
    - action_type: created|assigned|escalated|status_changed|comment|exported
    - payload (JSON) (ex: {fromSeverity,toSeverity,reason})
    - actor_id
    - created_at
- SQLite compat: migrations via sqlite_compat.py comme d’habitude.

2) Backend: endpoints incidents
- ORGA endpoints (partner portal namespace):
  A) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/incidents
     - list incidents (latest first), include assigned label
  B) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/incidents
     body: { description, severity, location?, title? }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - creates incident + log created
     - audit log entry
  C) POST /partner/portal/orga/incidents/{incident_id}/assign
     body: { assignmentId? role? reason? }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - log action assigned
     - audit log
  D) POST /partner/portal/orga/incidents/{incident_id}/escalate
     body: { severity, reason }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - severity change requires reason
     - log + audit
  E) POST /partner/portal/orga/incidents/{incident_id}/set_status
     body: { status, reason? }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - log + audit
  F) GET /partner/portal/orga/incidents/{incident_id}/logs
     - return action logs
  G) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/incidents/export
     - returns JSON or CSV (choose one) containing:
       incidents + logs + timestamps + actors (masked id ok) + current event state snapshot
- Rate limit: light, not strict.

3) Backend tests
- tests/test_incidents.py:
  - create incident idempotent
  - escalate requires reason
  - assign logs action
  - export returns incidents+logs

4) Portal UI: Incident module
- Ajouter une tuile “Incidents” dans EventControlHub.
- Nouvelle page /orga/events/:eventId/incidents :
  - list incidents (chips severity/status)
  - CTA “New incident” opens modal:
    - severity selector
    - description (required)
    - location (optional)
  - click incident -> IncidentDetail page or modal:
    - history/log timeline
    - assign (dropdown staff assignments fetched from /staff)
    - escalate (severity + reason)
    - set status (resolved/canceled)
  - Export button (download JSON/CSV)
- UX “terrain-friendly”:
  - gros labels, pas de surcharge
  - auto refresh léger (poll 3-5s) si utile
  - si offline/degraded: disable writes + show reason (réutiliser gate réseau existant)

5) Intégration avec staff

Prompt Codex — PR16 (P3.2 Orders Live complet)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P3.1 (Bar Dashboard) est livré. Prochaine étape: PR16 = P3.2 “Orders Live complet” pour opérer le bar.

OBJECTIF PR16 (P3.2)
- Implémenter dans le portal BAR MODE un écran “Orders Live” opérationnel:
  - liste temps réel des commandes (pending/preparing/ready/claimed/canceled)
  - actions: accept -> preparing, ready, claim (et cancel si autorisé)
  - cohérence avec l’app USER:
    - OrderStatusLiveScreen côté user doit refléter les mêmes statuts
- Respecter gating réseau + état event:
  - FROZEN/CLOSED => aucun write
  - PAUSE/MENU_ONLY => selon règles: commandes bloquées (au moins new orders + transitions)
  - DEGRADED => bloquer writes critiques (ou autoriser uniquement transitions idempotentes avec confirmation)
- Latence/terrain: action feedback immédiat (<1s) + optimistic UI possible mais sûr.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Write-sensible:
  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire sur toutes transitions
  - Audit log sur toutes transitions
  - Concurrence: éviter double-accept/double-claim (optimistic locking ou checks).
- Pas de refactor massif du modèle orders: réutiliser.
- Pas de P3.3 menu admin ici.
- UI dense: utilisable en service.

TÂCHES
1) Backend: endpoints Orders Live (bar-scoped)
- Vérifier endpoints existants (orders router).
- Ajouter/standardiser endpoints bar ops:
  A) GET /partner/portal/bar/events/{event_id}/orders/live
     query: status=all|pending|preparing|ready|claimed|canceled
     returns list compact:
       { id, createdAt, status, totalTickets, itemsSummary, customerLabel(masked), expiresAt(optional) }
  B) POST /partner/portal/bar/orders/{order_id}/accept
     body: { reason? } (optional)
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - transitions: pending -> preparing
  C) POST /partner/portal/bar/orders/{order_id}/ready
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - transitions: preparing -> ready
  D) POST /partner/portal/bar/orders/{order_id}/claim
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - transitions: ready -> claimed
  E) POST /partner/portal/bar/orders/{order_id}/cancel
     body: { reason } (required)
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - transitions: pending|preparing -> canceled (if allowed)
- Enforcement:
  - Role BAR_STAFF/MANAGER required AND order belongs to event’s bar.
  - EventState guards:
    - FROZEN/CLOSED => 403
    - MENU_ONLY/PAUSE => define rule; simplest:
      - block accept/ready/claim? (claim maybe allowed to clear backlog)
      - choose consistent: allow claim, block accept/ready if PAUSE, allow ready/claim if already in progress.
    - DEGRADED => allow only idempotent-safe transitions with confirmation OR block all writes.
      - Minimal safe: block accept/cancel, allow ready/claim (to finish service) with reason optional.
- Concurrency:
  - If current status does not match expected transition => return 409 with code (ORDER_STATE_CONFLICT) and current status.
- Audit logs for all transitions.
- Idempotency table for each transition or shared idempotency keyed by (order_id, action, key).

2) Backend tests
- tests/test_orders_live_bar_ops.py:
  - list live orders returns correct statuses
  - accept idempotent returns same
  - conflict returns 409 if already accepted
  - ready/claim transitions ok
  - cancel requires reason
  - permissions enforced

3) Portal: Orders Live UI
- Route: /bar/events/:eventId/orders
- Access:
  - only bar roles
- UI layout:
  - Header “Orders Live” + event badge + network badge + refresh
  - Tabs/filters chips: All / Pending / Preparing / Ready / Claimed
  - List dense of order cards:
    - left: time (mono) + order id short
    - middle: itemsSummary (2 lines max)
    - right: totalTickets + status chip
    - CTA row (contextual):
      - if pending: [Accept] [Cancel]
      - if preparing: [Ready] [Cancel]
      - if ready: [Claim]
      - if claimed/canceled: disabled, maybe collapse
  - Optimistic UI:
    - on click, set local “pendingAction” state, disable buttons until response
    - if 409, refresh row and show toast/banner message
- Polling:
  - 2s default online
  - pause polling offline
  - if degraded, poll slower (5s)
- Error handling:
  - use global network gate; local errors should be “Action failed, retry” not custom network banners.

4) User consistency
- Ensure backend order status mapping matches app OrderStatusLiveScreen mapping (pending/preparing/ready/claimed/canceled).
- If backend uses different internal strings, normalize in response.
- Update app api.dart or mapping only if needed.

DoD PR16

- Idempotency + audit on all writes.
- Conflicts handled (409) without breaking UI.
- Polling live + filters.
- User order status screen stays coherent.

Prompt Codex — PR19 (P4.1 Wallet manquants : Gifts P2P + Privacy toggles)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P2 (chapi-ready) et P3 (bar mode) sont complets. Prochaine étape: Phase P4. PR19 = P4.1 “Wallet manquants” (P2P gifts propre + consentement identité visible + toggles profil).

OBJECTIF PR19 (P4.1)
1) Gifts P2P (si module ON):
- Un user peut envoyer des tickets à un autre user:
  - via QR (scan receiver) OU via username/code court
  - idempotent, audit, anti-abus (rate limit)
  - UX claire: confirmation + reçu/échec
- Compatible event-scoped: si l’event ou le bar impose “identité visible”, respecter les settings.

2) Privacy/Consent:
- Ajouter un consentement explicite:
  - “Mon identité est visible dans ce bar/event”
- Ajouter des toggles profil:
  - public / friends / private (v1 minimal: public vs private suffit)
- Ces settings doivent impacter:
  - affichage du label user dans Orders Live / exports (masking)
  - affichage dans ticker (gifts/bets) si on les injecte plus tard

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Module ON/OFF:
  - Si gifts OFF: aucune UI gift
  - Si privacy OFF: garder defaults sans UI
- Write-sensible: QR signés + expiration + idempotency + audit log
- Réseau down: mode lecture => aucun gift possible (pas d’offline queue ici)
- Sécurité:
  - rate limit gifts (ex 5/min) + cooldown
  - block self-transfer
  - validation solde
  - ledger immuable (append-only)

TÂCHES
A) Backend: ledger + transfers
- Vérifier système wallet existant (TicketBalance etc.)
- Ajouter modèle TicketTransfer (ledger):
  - id, from_user_id, to_user_id, amount, event_id(optional), reason(optional), created_at
  - idempotency key table for transfers
- Endpoints:
  1) POST /wallet/transfers
     body: { toUserId OR toQrToken OR toCode, amount, eventId? }
     headers: Idempotency-Key required
     - checks: amount >0, balance sufficient, not self
     - rate limit
     - writes ledger + updates balances atomically
     - audit log
  2) GET /wallet/transfers (history)
- Receiver lookup:
  - Option QR: create a “ReceiveGift QR” payload signed with expiry containing receiver userId
  - Option code: short code per user (generated) or event-scoped code
- Tests backend:
  - transfer success updates balances
  - idempotent replay no double charge
  - insufficient funds => 400
  - rate limit => 429

B) Backend: privacy settings
- Model UserPrivacySettings:
  - user_id, visibility (public/private), show_identity_in_bar (bool), updated_at
- Endpoints:
  - GET /me/privacy
  - PUT /me/privacy (idempotent) body { visibility, showIdentityInBar }
- Ensure masking:
  - In bar orders live payload, if receiver privacy is private, customerLabel masked.
  - Exports already “masked minimal” but ensure consistent.

C) Flutter USER: Gift flow + privacy UI
- WalletScreen:
  - If gifts module ON: add section “Send gift”
    - scan receiver QR (use scanner universal) OR enter code
    - amount picker
    - confirm sheet (recipient masked if private)
    - submit -> success state + update balance/history
  - Add Privacy section:
    - toggle visibility public/private
    - toggle “identity visible in this bar” if relevant (event/bar context)
- Respect write gate:
  - offline/degraded/read-only => disable send with reason.
- Add ReceiveGift QR:
  - new screen “My gift QR” showing signed QR that others can scan.

D) Portal (optional in PR19)
- If needed, display privacy mask in Orders Live already done. If backend handles, portal just shows returned label.

DoD PR19
- Gifts P2P works end-to-end + idempotent + audited + rate limited.
- Privacy settings saved and affect masking in bar orders payload.
- UI respects module ON/OFF + gating.
- Tests backend green.

Prompt Codex — PR20 (P4.2 Ticker Live étendu + multi-types + états)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P4.1 (Wallet gifts + privacy) est livré. Prochaine étape: PR20 = P4.2 “Ticker Live étendu”.

OBJECTIF PR20 (P4.2)
- Transformer le Ticker en feed multi-types cohérent et robust:
  - types à supporter maintenant (minimum):
    1) broadcast (déjà)
    2) order (déjà partiel)
    3) price change (si price history existe)
    4) gift transfer (nouveau P4.1)
  - types “placeholder” (sans implémenter features complètes) mais prêts:
    battles, hunt, bets (polymarket), crash/alerts
- Respect strict des modules ON/OFF:
  - un type ne doit apparaître que si le module correspondant est ON.
  - ex: gifts -> seulement si gifts module ON
  - broadcast -> seulement si broadcast module ON (déjà strict)
- Respect strict des états runtime event:
  - PAUSE/MENU_ONLY/FROZEN/DEGRADED doit influencer:
    - write actions (déjà)
    - feed coherence: si offline/degraded => mode lecture + cache; pas de “trous”
- UX:
  - afficher quelque chose même offline (cache)
  - refresh stable (poll + dedup)
  - filtres (déjà existants) doivent rester compatibles et inclure Gifts/Alerts si nécessaire

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Pas de WebSocket obligatoire (v1 = polling).
- Déduplication obligatoire:
  - même item ne s’affiche pas 2x entre refresh
  - pagination/“since” recommandé
- Privacy:
  - gifts doivent respecter privacy (mask display) comme orders.
- Performance:
  - feed payload compact (max 100 items).
  - polling adaptatif (2s online, 5s degraded, stop offline).

TÂCHES
1) Backend: ticker aggregator unifié
- Créer/étendre endpoint:
  A) GET /events/{event_id}/ticker
     query:
       - since (timestamp or cursor) optional
       - limit (default 50, max 100)
       - types=comma optional (filter)
     returns:
       {
         items: [
           { id, type, ts, payloadCompact, severity?, moduleKey }
         ],
         nextCursor,
         serverTime
       }
- Sources:
  - broadcast (already in ticker router)
  - orders events:
     - order_created / status_changed
     - payload minimal: orderId short, status, totalTickets, masked user label optional
  - price events:
     - from PriceHistory / EventDrink changes
     - payload: drinkName, fromPrice, toPrice, direction, ts
  - gift events:
     - from TicketTransfer ledger
     - payload: amount, fromLabel(masked), toLabel(masked), ts
- Modules gating:
  - if module OFF, exclude at aggregation time (strict).
- States:
  - ticker read-only always, but if event FROZEN maybe still show (choose: still show, read-only)
- Dedup IDs:
  - set id = stable key per item (ex: "gift:<transferId>", "broadcast:<id>", "order:<orderId>:<status>:<ts>", "price:<historyId>")
- Add tests backend: tests/test_ticker_extended.py
  - when gifts module ON -> gifts appear
  - when OFF -> gifts excluded
  - privacy masking applied
  - since filter returns only newer

2) Flutter: ticker models + repository
- Create typed models:
  - TickerItem { id, type, ts, payload }
  - payload unions for broadcast/order/price/gift
- Repository:
  - fetchTicker(eventId, cursor/since, types)
  - merge + dedup in provider
  - cache last N in SharedPreferences (or existing cache layer)
- Polling:
  - online 2s
  - degraded 5s
  - offline stop polling, show cache + label last sync
- Event state integration:
  - if eventState changes, reflect banner and keep ticker consistent (no reset unless eventId changes)

3) UI: TickerScreen enhancements
- Keep existing filter chips:
  - extend with “Gifts” and “Prices” if not present
  - map to backend types param if used
- Render:
  - broadcast card (existing)
  - gift card (new): “X sent Y tickets to Z” masked + small icon + mono ts
  - price card: “Beer ↑ 3→4” with direction chip
  - order card: “Order ready” etc.
- Make it dense and readable (Nothing-like).
- Ensure: module OFF => no filter chip, no items.

DoD PR20
- Backend ticker returns multi-types with since + dedup stable IDs.
- Gifts appear only when module ON and privacy masked.
- App shows cache offline and doesn’t duplicate.
- Filters work and don’t show modules OFF.

OBJECTIF PR22 (P5.2)

- Fonctionnalités:

  - PAUSE/MENU_ONLY => buy peut être bloqué (choisir règle stricte: bloque buy/create/resolve)

     returns market

     - permission: resolver role (manager/owner)
     - enforce module ON
     - reason required
     - audit log resolve
     - lock market (status resolved)

ChatGPT a dit :
Nickel. P5.4 est livré (favoris offline, rupture cohérente, blocage panier, total estimé + affiché QR).
👉 Phase P5 = COMPLETE.

Tu veux maintenant P6 Musique mais en ajoutant YouTube Music + SoundCloud en plus de Spotify/Apple. Go — on le structure pour rester réaliste et robuste.

P6 Musique : approche “provider-agnostic” (Spotify/Apple/YT Music/SoundCloud)
Le point dur : YouTube Music et Apple Music n’ont pas une API “queue-control” aussi ouverte que Spotify. SoundCloud a une API plus simple, mais les droits/permissions varient.
Donc on fait une architecture Music Providers + modes :

Mode A — “Vote + Playback externe” (universel)
Le public vote, TORÈ calcule “Next track”, et le DJ/owner lance manuellement via un lien (deep link / search).
✅ Marche pour Spotify / Apple / YouTube Music / SoundCloud
✅ Simple, légal, robuste

Mode B — “Auto-queue” (quand API le permet)
Spotify peut permettre d’ajouter à la queue selon scopes et device.
Apple/YT Music généralement non (ou très limité / privé).
✅ Optionnel, pas obligatoire pour V1.

On livre Mode A en PR25 (vraiment utilisable en soirée), et on garde Spotify auto-queue en PR26 si tu veux.

Prompt Codex — PR25 (P6 Musique V1 : Vote universel + DJ/Owner override + providers)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P5 est complet. Prochaine étape: P6 Musique. PR25 = “Music Vote V1” avec providers Spotify + Apple Music + YouTube Music + SoundCloud.

OBJECTIF PR25 (P6)
- Livrer un module “Music vote” event-scoped:
  - Public vote pour la prochaine track
  - Owner active/désactive le module
  - Owner peut activer “DJ override”:
    - DJ peut forcer un track
    - DJ peut ignorer les votes / reset vote
  - Multi-provider: Spotify / Apple Music / YouTube Music / SoundCloud
- V1 = provider-agnostic “Vote + launch link”:
  - TORÈ ne contrôle pas forcément la lecture; TORÈ fournit “Next track winner” + liens.
  - DJ/owner lance manuellement (un bouton “Open in …”).
- Respect modules ON/OFF:
  - si music_vote OFF => aucune trace UI (EventHub).
- Réseau:
  - offline/degraded => lecture seule (votes bloqués)
- Write-sensible:
  - idempotency + audit log sur vote, force track, toggle module, DJ override changes
  - anti-spam vote: 1 vote / 10s / user + cooldown
- Privacy:
  - les votes n’exposent pas d’identités; seulement counts.

ARCHITECTURE (IMPORTANT)
- Créer un modèle “TrackCandidate” provider-agnostic:
  { id, provider: spotify|apple|ytmusic|soundcloud, title, artist, artworkUrl?, durationSec?, externalUrl, providerUri?, searchHint }
- Une “MusicSession” par event:
  - enabled(bool)
  - providerDefault (enum) + allowMixedProviders(bool) v1 true/false
  - djOverrideEnabled(bool)
  - currentQueueWinner (TrackCandidate? + decidedAt)
  - votes: map trackId->count
  - lastUpdatedAt
- V1: pas d’auth OAuth obligatoire pour user.
  - DJ peut choisir provider à l’ouverture.

TÂCHES BACKEND
1) Modèles + persistence
- Ajouter tables:
  - event_music_settings (event_id, enabled, dj_override_enabled, allow_mixed, updated_at, updated_by)
  - event_music_candidates (id, event_id, provider, external_url, title, artist, artwork_url, created_at) (optional cache)
  - event_music_votes (event_id, user_id, candidate_key, created_at)
  - event_music_actions_audit (append-only) OR reuse audit log system existant
  - idempotency tables for vote + admin actions (vote_idempotency, music_admin_idempotency)
- Candidate key stable:
  - provider + ":" + normalized externalUrl (or providerId)

2) Endpoints
A) GET /events/{event_id}/music
   returns:
    { enabled, djOverrideEnabled, allowMixedProviders, providerDefault, winner, topCandidates[], lastUpdatedAt }
   - if module music_vote OFF => return 404 MODULE_OFF or empty with enabled=false (choose strict: 404 MODULE_OFF)

B) POST /events/{event_id}/music/vote
   body: { candidate: TrackCandidate(minimal: provider, externalUrl, title, artist, artworkUrl?) }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   - guards: module ON, enabled true, runtime state allows writes, offline not relevant server-side
   - anti-spam: cooldown per user
   - stores vote; recompute top list
   - audit log vote

C) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/settings
   body: { enabled?, djOverrideEnabled?, allowMixedProviders?, providerDefault? }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   - permissions: ORGA manager/owner
   - reason optional but store audit
   - apply

D) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/force
   body: { candidate: TrackCandidate, reason }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   - permissions: ORGA manager/owner OR DJ role if djOverrideEnabled
   - sets winner immediately, clears votes optionally (v1 keep votes but set winner)

   - permissions as force
   - clears votes + winner
   - audit

La suite logique pour rendre ça vraiment “soirée-ready” : PR26 = “Rounds + Now Playing + timer + DJ controls”.
Parce qu’en vrai, en soirée, il faut :

des fenêtres de vote (ex: toutes les 3 min)

un winner verrouillé jusqu’au prochain round

un contrôle DJ/owner simple : Start round / End round / Skip / Force / Clear

OBJECTIF PR26
- Ajouter un système de rounds (fenêtres de vote) event-scoped:
  - Owner/DJ démarre un round (durée configurable: 60/120/180/300s)
  - Les users votent pendant le round
  - À la fin (auto ou manual close), winner est “locked”
  - UI Now Playing / Next Winner / Next round timer
- Ajouter un panneau DJ/Owner (dans l’app, event-scoped):
  - Start round / End round
  - Set “Now Playing” (optionnel, via link/candidate)
  - Skip winner (clear winner and reopen round)
  - Force track (déjà) + Clear votes (déjà)
- Garder provider-agnostic: toujours “Open in …” (pas d’auto queue obligatoire)
- Respect runtime + network gating: offline/degraded/read-only => aucun write
- Respect DJ override flag.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Write-sensible: idempotency + audit sur start/end/nowPlaying/skip
- Anti-abus:
  - vote cooldown déjà présent
  - empêcher votes hors round si enabledRoundOnly=true (v1 yes)
- Module OFF => 404 MODULE_OFF (strict) + aucune UI
- Performance: polling léger:
  - users: 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline
  - admin panel: 2s online / 5s degraded / stop offline

BACKEND — DATA MODEL
- Ajouter état de session (event_music_session):
  - event_id
  - enabled
  - dj_override_enabled
  - allow_mixed
  - provider_default
  - round:
      round_id (uuid)
      status: OPEN|LOCKED|IDLE
      started_at
      ends_at
      duration_sec
      locked_at
  - now_playing: TrackCandidate? (provider/url/title/artist/artwork)
  - winner: TrackCandidate? + locked_at + votesCount
  - last_updated_at
- Conserver votes/candidates existants; ajouter:
  - event_music_round_idempotency table (or reuse admin idempotency with action keys)

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
A) GET /events/{event_id}/music
   - enrichir réponse:
     { enabled, round{status, startedAt, endsAt, durationSec}, nowPlaying?, winner?, topCandidates[], lastUpdatedAt }

B) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/round/start
   body: { durationSec } (allowed values: 60/120/180/300)
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if djOverrideEnabled
   guard: runtime allows writes
   behavior:
     - creates new round_id
     - status OPEN
     - ends_at = now + duration
     - clears votes? (recommended yes: clear votes for new round)
     - audit

C) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/round/end
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - locks winner based on current votes
   - status LOCKED
   - sets winner + locked_at
   - audit

D) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/now-playing
   body: { candidate: TrackCandidate, reason? }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - sets now_playing
   - audit

E) POST /events/{event_id}/music/admin/skip
   body: { reason }
   headers: Idempotency-Key required
   perms: manager/owner OR DJ if override enabled
   - clears winner and sets status IDLE (or re-open round if you prefer)
   - audit

F) Vote rule update:
- POST /events/{event_id}/music/vote should:
  - reject votes if round.status != OPEN (HTTP 409 VOTE_CLOSED)
  - or allow if you choose, but V2 should be round-only. Choose strict round-only.

G) (Optional) background auto-lock:
- simplest: no background job; client can call end round when timer reached.
- but GET /music should compute if now>ends_at and status OPEN => treat as LOCKED and lock lazily server-side (safe). Implement “lazy finalize” inside GET /music or on vote call.

  - “anonyme” n’existe pas ici (forum interne), mais respecter privacy globale? => ici on affiche le nom comité (car privé). Si user visibility private, on peut quand même afficher un pseudonyme interne (optionnel). V1: afficher displayName normal.

BACKEND — MODELS
- committee_forum_categories:
  - id, committee_id, year_id, name, slug, order_index, is_archived
- committee_forum_posts:
  - id, committee_id, year_id, category_id
  - title, body
  - created_by_user_id, created_at
  - updated_at
  - is_pinned, is_locked
  - is_deleted (soft delete), deleted_at, deleted_by
- committee_forum_post_actions_idempotency (or reuse committee_admin_idempotency with action scope)
- (optional) committee_forum_comments (skip if too large; ok to omit)

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Public (member-only access required via membership):
A) GET /committees/{committee_id}/forum/categories?year_id=...
   returns list sorted order_index
B) GET /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts?year_id=...&category_id=...&cursor&limit
   - pinned first, then latest
C) GET /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}
D) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts
   body { yearId, categoryId, title, body }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - require permission can_post_forum (default true for MEMBER+)
   - rate limit
E) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/pin (MOD+)
   body { pinned: bool, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
F) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/lock (MOD+)
   body { locked: bool, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
G) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/posts/{post_id}/delete (MOD+)
   body { reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
Admin categories (ADMIN/OWNER):
H) POST /committees/{committee_id}/forum/categories
   body { yearId, name, orderIndex? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
I) PUT /committees/{committee_id}/forum/categories/{category_id}
   body { name?, orderIndex?, isArchived? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required

GUARDS / VALIDATIONS
- Ensure membership active and year belongs to committee
- Ensure category belongs to committee/year
- Soft delete: posts hidden from list but accessible to MOD/ADMIN for audit? V1: hide entirely for non-mod.
- Cursor pagination: can be created_at timestamp + id.

IDEMPOTENCY RULES

Centralized QR signing/verification with key-ring rotation support (TORE_QR_SIGNING_KEYS) and enforced strict signed-QR validation in /qr/resolve for sensitive types (signature + expiry + token lookup + revoked), with explicit legacy=1 escape hatch. 

Updated QR pack generation to use the centralized signing service and documented the new signing key env variable. 

Added capstone tests for write-sensible hardening and signed QR resolve status matrix (ok/expired/invalid/revoked).

Prompt Codex — PR31 (P8.1 WiFi via QR : token lifecycle, revoke, offline read-only)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P9.1 capstone write-sensible est livré. Prochaine étape: PR31 = P8.1 “WiFi via QR” (module indépendant ON/OFF).

OBJECTIF PR31
- Ajouter un module WiFi event-scoped accessible via QR:
  - ORGA génère un QR WiFi (signed + expiring) dans le QR pack event
  - USER scanne -> obtient soit:
    A) un portail “WiFi info” (SSID + password) si autorisé
    B) OU un token temporaire (recommended) avec expiration
  - ORGA peut REVOKE un token (lifecycle)
- Respect strict:
  - module OFF => aucune UI + resolve renvoie module_off
  - offline => mode lecture (pas de génération/revoke, mais lecture cache)
  - write-sensible: idempotency + audit log
  - QR signed + expiry (utiliser qr_signing_service introduit PR30)

SCOPE V1 (réaliste)
- Pas d’intégration routeur/Unifi, pas de config WiFi automatique OS-level.
- Juste “distribution contrôlée” des infos via QR + token.
- Option: afficher “SSID + pass” seulement si token valide, sinon refuser.

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_wifi_settings:
  - event_id
  - enabled (bool)
  - ssid (string)
  - password (string) (store as encrypted? V1: plain in DB but never in logs)
  - instructions (optional)
  - updated_at, updated_by
- event_wifi_tokens:
  - id
  - event_id
  - token_hash (sha256)
  - created_at
  - expires_at
  - revoked_at, revoked_by, revoke_reason
  - issued_to_user_id (optional)
- event_wifi_idempotency (or reuse admin idempotency)

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Admin ORGA (manager/owner):
A) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi
   returns { enabled, ssid, hasPassword(true/false), instructions, tokenStats(active/revoked/expired) }
B) PUT /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi
   body { enabled, ssid?, password?, instructions? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - audit log (do NOT log password value)
C) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi/tokens
   body { ttlSeconds } default 3600, allowed 300..86400
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - returns { tokenId, token, expiresAt }
   - audit (no token in audit)
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/wifi/tokens/{token_id}/revoke
   body { reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - audit

Public USER (event-scoped):
E) GET /events/{event_id}/wifi/claim
   query: token=...
   - validates: module ON, token exists, not expired, not revoked
   - returns { ssid, password, instructions, expiresAt }
   - privacy: do not expose other tokens; just this claim response
   - rate-limit claim attempts

QR INTEGRATION
- Add a new QrType: WIFI_EVENT
- ORGA QR pack includes a signed deeplink:
  tore://qr?type=wifi_event&eventId=...&expiresAt=...&sig=...
- /qr/resolve for WIFI_EVENT must enforce signed QR validation (using PR30 services)
- When USER scans WIFI_EVENT:
  - App routes to WifiScreen:
    - fetches event wifi claim flow (needs a token)
  - BUT the QR itself should not include the password.
  - Flow:
    1) QR opens WifiLandingScreen with “Request WiFi access”
    2) ORGA generates tokens and displays another QR “WiFi token” OR shares token string
    => Too complex.
  - Better v1:
    - WIFI_EVENT QR itself carries a short-lived token generated in advance.
    - So QR pack includes WIFI token QR (rotated by ORGA as needed)
    - Implement this as: ORGA endpoint creates token + returns a QR payload with token embedded.
    - QR type: WIFI_TOKEN (signed) with tokenId + token + expiry.

Therefore:
- Add QrType.WIFI_TOKEN, payload includes eventId + token (or tokenId + token) + expiresAt + sig
- /qr/resolve must validate signature and then validate token lifecycle.

Update QR pack generation:
- include WIFI_TOKEN QR only if wifi module enabled
- generate 1 default token TTL 6h (configurable later)

TESTS BACKEND
- tests/test_wifi_qr_tokens.py
  - module off behavior
  - create token idempotent
  - claim ok, expired, revoked
  - audit logs created without password/token leakage
  - qr resolve signed enforcement for WIFI_TOKEN

PORTAL UI (ORGA)
- In EventControlHub add tile “WiFi”
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/wifi:
  - enable toggle
  - ssid + password fields
  - “Generate QR token” (ttl select)
  - list latest tokens (status active/expired/revoked) + revoke
  - copy deeplink/QR payload for printing
  - write gating offline/degraded/runtime

FLUTTER UI (USER)
- Add WifiScreen:
  - opened from QR router for WIFI_TOKEN
  - calls /events/{event_id}/wifi/claim?token=...
  - displays SSID + password + instructions
  - shows expiry countdown (optional)
  - offline: if cached last claim, show cached with “mode lecture”
- Ensure module ON/OFF:
  - if wifi module OFF, QR router goes to ModuleUnavailableScreen

SECURITY
- Never log wifi password or raw token in audit logs.
- Store token as hash in DB.
- Rate limit claim endpoint.

DoD PR31
- ORGA peut activer WiFi, config SSID/pass, générer QR token, révoquer.
- USER scan -> voit WiFi info si token valide.
- Signed QR enforced + lifecycle enforced.
- Tests pass.

Prompt Codex — PR32 (P8.2 Hunts QR : création, scan, rewards, classement, defend)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. P8.1 WiFi QR est livré. Prochaine étape: PR32 = P8.2 “Hunts QR + cagnottes défendables” (module indépendant ON/OFF, event-scoped).

OBJECTIF PR32 (Hunt V1 jouable)
- Un event peut activer le module Hunt (modulesOn.hunt=true).
- ORGA crée une “Hunt” (une chasse) avec:
  - titre, description, période active, règles
  - une liste de checkpoints QR (ex: 10 QR cachés)
  - chaque checkpoint donne des points + éventuellement une récompense (tickets / badge)
- USER scanne un QR checkpoint:
  - validation signée + expiry (QR sensibles)
  - idempotent: un user ne peut valider 2 fois le même checkpoint
  - retour immédiat “+X points” + feedback <1s
- Leaderboard Hunt:
  - top3 + list dense + me
- Defendable pot (v1 simple):
  - chaque user possède un “pot” de points de la hunt
  - une action “steal/defend” possible 1 fois / 10 min:
    - user A peut tenter de voler une fraction (ex 10%) à user B si B est “visible” (ou via QR duel)
  - V1 safe: pas de P2P en offline, runtime guards stricts, audit logs

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Module OFF => aucune UI + endpoints 404 MODULE_OFF
- Write-sensible:
  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire sur scan checkpoint + actions steal/defend + creation admin
  - Audit logs sur toutes actions
- QR: signés + expiration + resolve strict (utiliser qr_signing_service / qr_validation_service)
- Réseau:
  - offline => mode lecture (pas de scan write)
  - degraded => bloquer writes critiques (scan & steal sont critiques)
- Performance terrain:
  - scan -> feedback UX <1s (optimiser backend path, minimal writes)
- Privacy:
  - leaderboard masque les users private (Anonymous-xxxx), mais “me” toujours visible

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_hunts:
  - id, event_id, title, description, starts_at, ends_at, is_active
  - rules_text
  - created_by, created_at
- event_hunt_checkpoints:
  - id, hunt_id, label, points, reward_tickets (optional)
  - qr_token_id (links generated tokens)
  - order_index
- event_hunt_claims:
  - id, hunt_id, checkpoint_id, user_id
  - claimed_at
  - points_awarded
- event_hunt_scores:
  - hunt_id, user_id, score (cached aggregate), last_updated_at
- event_hunt_actions_idempotency (or reuse existing idempotency service with scope keys)
- event_hunt_steals:
  - id, hunt_id, attacker_user_id, victim_user_id
  - amount, created_at, result (OK|BLOCKED|COOLDOWN|VICTIM_PRIVATE)
  - audit

QR TYPES

- /qr/resolve must enforce signed validation for hunt_checkpoint
- QR pack generation for Hunt should be in ORGA portal hunt admin (not in event create pack by default).

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Admin ORGA (manager/owner):
A) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/hunts
   body { title, description, startsAt, endsAt, rulesText, checkpoints:[{label,points,rewardTickets?,orderIndex}] }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - creates hunt + checkpoints
   - generates signed QR tokens per checkpoint (TTL default endsAt)
   - audit
B) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/hunts
C) GET /partner/portal/orga/hunts/{hunt_id}/qr_pack
   returns list {checkpoint,label,points,deeplink,expiresAt} + printable view in portal
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/hunts/{hunt_id}/close
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - sets inactive, audit

USER:
E) GET /events/{event_id}/hunts/active
   returns current hunt + brief checkpoints count + endsAt + myScore
F) POST /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/claim
   body { checkpointId, token }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - validates module ON
   - validates token hash + expiry + not revoked (like wifi)
   - ensures checkpoint belongs to hunt+event
   - idempotent: if already claimed -> return stable response (alreadyClaimed=true)
   - updates score aggregate
   - returns { ok, pointsAwarded, totalScore, rewardTicketsAwarded? }
   - audit
G) GET /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/leaderboard
   returns top3 + list + me (mask privacy)

H) POST /events/{event_id}/hunts/{hunt_id}/steal
   body { victimUserId }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - cooldown 10 min per attacker
   - deny if victim privacy=private (unless victim==me)
   - stealAmount = floor(victimScore*0.1) min 1 max 50
   - update both scores
   - audit
   - returns { ok, amount, attackerScore, victimScoreMasked? }

  - stealHunt(eventId, huntId, victimUserId) (Idempotency-Key)
- Ajouter modèles typed pour réponses.

B) Providers (Riverpod)
- activeHuntProvider(eventId) polling (2s online, 5s degraded, stop offline)
- huntLeaderboardProvider(eventId, huntId) polling léger (5s online, 10s degraded)
- local cache SharedPreferences:
  - cache key: hunt_active_{eventId}
  - hunt_lb_{eventId}_{huntId}
  - store payload + lastSyncAt + TTL 24h

C) UI Screens
1) HuntScreen (route depuis EventHub)
- Header: Hunt title + countdown + chip ACTIVE/ENDED
- ToreCard “My score” (score + lastClaim info)
- CTA primary “Scan checkpoint”
  - opens universal QrScannerScreen
  - on scan: if payload type hunt_checkpoint:
      - validate eventId matches active event (sinon message)
      - call claim endpoint immediately
      - show ClaimResultSheet (big)
  - If scanned QR not hunt_checkpoint: show QrStatusScreen (existing)
- Secondary: button “Leaderboard”

2) ClaimResultSheet
- success: big +points + totalScore
- alreadyClaimed: show “Déjà validé” + totalScore
- error: show reason (expired/invalid/revoked/offline/runtime blocked)
- Add haptic + sound (reuse existing)

3) HuntLeaderboardScreen
- Top3 cards (premium)
- List dense (ToreListRow) with “Steal” trailing action button for each row (except me)
- When press Steal:
  - confirm dialog
  - call steal endpoint
  - show result snack (+amount or error)
  - cooldown UI: disable button if backend says cooldownRemaining (if not provided, just show error message)

4) QR Router integration
- When scanning hunt_checkpoint anywhere (global scanner):
  - route to HuntClaim handler:
    - if no active event context or mismatch => show QrInvalidScreen with explanation
    - else push HuntScreen and auto-run claim (or show modal claim result directly)
- Prefer: open modal claim result without deep navigation if user already in event.

D) EventHub wiring
- Ensure event_hub_screen.dart module card opens HuntScreen when key 'hunt' ON.
- Keep modules ON-only rule.

OBJECTIF PR34
- Un module Battles (alias “Affonds”) qui permet:
  1) ORGA/DJ lance une “battle round” (OPEN/LOCKED/IDLE) avec thème + durée.
  2) Users participent en 1 tap pendant OPEN:
     - soit “Je participe” (simple)
     - soit “Vote” (TEAM A / TEAM B) si round est de type duel
  3) Résultat affiché live + winner + historique court.
  4) Classement battles (score) : top3 + liste dense + me.
- Option folklore: “Duc de l’Affond” = badge du #1 du round, affiché dans UI.

CONTRAINTES NON NÉGOCIABLES
- Module OFF => endpoints 404 MODULE_OFF et aucune entrée UI.
- Writes critiques (start/end/vote/participate/force winner/clear) :
  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire (utiliser ensure_idempotency_key)
  - audit logs systématiques
  - bloqués offline/degraded/runtime blocked (PAUSE/MENU_ONLY/DEGRADED/FROZEN/CLOSED)
- Privacy:
  - leaderboard masque users private (Anonymous-xxxx), me visible.
- Performance:
  - UX vote <1s: backend path minimal.
- Offline:
  - read-only avec cache (round state + last results + leaderboard)

BACKEND — MODELS
- event_battle_rounds:
  - id, event_id
  - title, kind: PARTICIPATION|DUEL
  - optionA_label, optionB_label (for DUEL)
  - status: IDLE|OPEN|LOCKED
  - starts_at, ends_at, locked_at
  - created_by, created_at
  - winner (A|B|NONE) + winner_count_a/b
- event_battle_votes:
  - id, round_id, user_id
  - choice: A|B|PARTICIPATE
  - created_at
  - unique(round_id,user_id) (idempotence logical)
- event_battle_scores:
  - event_id, user_id, score, last_updated_at
  - scoring v1:
    - participation in OPEN => +1
    - correct winner vote (DUEL) => +2 (if vote matches winner)
    - participation in winning side => +1 extra
- event_battle_idempotency
- (optional) event_battle_actions log table if needed; but audit service should cover actions.

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
USER:
A) GET /events/{event_id}/battles/state
   returns current round (if any), serverTime, myChoice, counts, status, endsAt
B) POST /events/{event_id}/battles/vote
   body { roundId, choice } where choice in [A,B,PARTICIPATE]
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - module guard
   - runtime guard
   - only allowed if round OPEN and now<endsAt
   - upsert vote (unique constraint)
   - returns { ok, myChoice, countsA, countsB, status }
C) GET /events/{event_id}/battles/leaderboard
   returns top3 + list + me + rulesText

ADMIN ORGA/DJ:
D) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/start
   body { title, kind, durationSeconds, optionALabel?, optionBLabel? }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - closes previous OPEN (lock)
   - starts new OPEN
   - audit (no sensitive)
E) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/lock
   body { roundId }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - lock now, compute winner for DUEL (max votes)
   - update scores (batch)
   - audit
F) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/rounds/force_winner
   body { roundId, winner:A|B|NONE, reason }
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - recompute scores accordingly
   - audit (reason required)
G) GET /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/history?limit=20
H) POST /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/battles/clear
   headers Idempotency-Key required
   - clears votes for current round (optional v1) or resets to IDLE
   - audit (reason required)

TESTS BACKEND: tests/test_battles_v1.py
- module off strict
- start round idempotent
- vote allowed only OPEN and before expiry
- lock computes winner + updates scores
- leaderboard masking privacy
- runtime blocked write behavior
- audit logs created
- idempotency required on write endpoints

Prompt Codex — PR37 (P8 : Carte & itinéraires + calendrier V1, offline-friendly)
Tu es Codex (agent dev autonome) sur TORÈ. Hunt + Battles + WiFi + Safe Kit + Comité OS + Music Vote sont livrés. Prochaine étape: PR37 = P8 “Carte & itinéraires + calendrier guindaille” V1.

OBJECTIF PR37 (V1 utile, adoption)
USER:
1) Module “Carte” dans Event Hub si modulesOn.map=true
   - affiche le lieu event (adresse + lat/lng si dispo)
   - boutons: Google Maps / Apple Maps / Waze / TEC (lien)
   - section “Navettes” (si configuré): départ/retour + last shuttle time
2) Module “Calendrier” accessible depuis Home (hors event) ou comité:
   - liste d’events publics (Live/Today/Upcoming) cross-bars
   - filtre: “Aujourd’hui / Semaine / Mois”
   - ajout au calendrier device (ICS) + partage
3) Offline:
   - mode lecture + cache 24h (event location + calendar list)
4) Scope strict:
   - pas de géolocalisation temps réel
   - pas de navigation turn-by-turn in-app
   - juste deep links vers apps externes + affichage infos

BACKEND (FastAPI) — endpoints minimal
A) Event location config:
   - GET /events/{event_id}/map
     returns { enabled, venueName?, address?, lat?, lng?, navettes?: { enabled, address?, lastReturnTime?, notes? }, tecLink?, wazeLink? }
   - Strict module OFF => 404 MODULE_OFF (modulesOn.map)
B) Public calendar:
   - GET /calendar/events?from=...&to=...&scope=public
     returns list of public events (id,name,startAt,endAt,venue,address,barId,barName,status)
   - pagination cursor optional
C) ICS:
   - GET /calendar/events/{event_id}.ics
     returns ICS text (VEVENT) with DTSTART/DTEND, location, summary, URL=deep link tore://event/{id}
   - no auth required if event is public
D) Portal ORGA config:
   - GET/PUT /partner/portal/orga/events/{event_id}/map/settings
     Idempotency-Key required on PUT
     fields: enabled, venueName, address, lat,lng, navettes config, tecLink/wazeLink
     audit logs (do not log full address if you consider it sensitive; ok to log “updated map settings” with event_id)

MODELS
- event_map_settings (event_id, enabled, venue_name, address, lat, lng, tec_link, waze_link)
- event_navettes_settings (event_id, enabled, address, last_return_time, notes)
- idempotency table event_map_idempotency (or reuse generic)

TESTS backend: tests/test_map_calendar_v1.py
- module off strict for /events/{id}/map
- calendar list returns expected shape
- ics endpoint returns a VEVENT with DTSTART/DTEND/LOCATION/SUMMARY
- map settings PUT requires idempotency + audit

PORTAL
- Add tile “Carte & Navettes” in EventControlHub when module map ON
- Page /orga/events/:eventId/map:
  - toggle enabled
  - venue name + address + lat/lng (optional)
  - navettes toggle + address + last return time + notes
  - quick preview card
  - write gating offline/degraded/runtime
  - save uses Idempotency-Key

FLUTTER
A) API
- add getEventMap(eventId)
- add getCalendarEvents(from,to)
- add getEventIcsUrl(eventId) OR just open backend URL in browser
B) Providers
- eventMapProvider(eventId) with cache TTL 24h
- calendarEventsProvider(range) with cache TTL 24h
C) UI
1) EventMapScreen (from Event Hub module “Carte”):
   - ToreCard venue/address + chips status
   - buttons:
     - Ouvrir Google Maps (geo:)
     - Ouvrir Apple Plans
     - Ouvrir Waze
     - Ouvrir TEC link (if provided)
   - Navettes card (if enabled): address + lastReturnTime + notes
   - Offline banner + last sync
2) CalendarScreen (entry from HomeHub when no active event, and optionally from CommitteeHome):
   - tabs/chips Today / Week / Month
   - list cards (event name, time, bar)
   - CTA “Ajouter au calendrier” -> open ICS url (url_launcher)
   - CTA “Rejoindre” -> route to BarEvents or JoinEvent flow if you have direct event id join (if not, just open EventHub placeholder with read-only)

DEPENDENCIES
- Use url_launcher (already added for tel:). Reuse it for external map apps + ICS.
- For geo links:
  - Google: https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=lat,lng OR encoded address
  - Apple Maps: http://maps.apple.com/?q=... or ll=lat,lng
  - Waze: https://waze.com/ul?ll=lat,lng&navigate=yes
  - TEC: use tecLink if provided

USER:

  - Home (hors event) si module folklore_glossary ON

COMITÉ (admin/mods):
- Créer / éditer / archiver un terme

  - soft delete (visible mods uniquement)
  - historique minimal (audit)

CONTRAINTES
- MODULE OFF => endpoints 404 MODULE_OFF + aucune UI

  - Idempotency-Key obligatoire
  - audit logs
  - anti-spam: 10 writes / 10 min / comité (simple rate-limit)
- Permissions:
  - membership required pour write
  - seules permissions canModerateForum OR committee role ADMIN/MOD peuvent éditer/supprimer
  - lecture publique possible si comité choisit “publicGlossary=true”

BACKEND — MODELS
- committee_glossary_settings:

  - created_at, created_by, updated_at, updated_by
- committee_glossary_idempotency

BACKEND — ENDPOINTS
Public (si settings.public_enabled):
A) GET /glossary/committees
   -> returns list committees where glossary public enabled
B) GET /glossary/committees/{committee_id}/terms?query=&tags=&year=&cursor=&limit<=100
   -> returns { items, nextCursor, serverTime }
C) GET /glossary/committees/{committee_id}/settings
   -> returns public_enabled + available tags (computed)

Private (membership required):
D) GET /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms?... (same as public but includes archived for mods)
E) POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms
   body { term, definition, examples?, tags?, yearId?, isOfficial? }
   Idempotency-Key required
F) PUT /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}
   body patch { definition?, examples?, tags?, isOfficial?, status? }
   Idempotency-Key required
G) POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}/archive (reason optional)
   Idempotency-Key required
H) POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}/delete (reason required)
   Idempotency-Key required
I) GET/PUT /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/settings
   PUT { public_enabled }
   Idempotency-Key required

Guards:
- module folklore_glossary must be ON globally (like committee_os gate)
- membership + role checks on writes
- rate limit 10 writes / 10 minutes per actor+committee
- audit logs for create/edit/archive/delete/settings

global module gate (TORE_MODULE_FOLKLORE_GLOSSARY) with strict 404 {reason: MODULE_OFF},

moderation/write authorization checks,

tag sanitation,

term payload shaping,

and a dedicated write rate-limit (10 writes / 10 minutes / actor+committee).

Extended /modules response to expose folklore_glossary state for clients to hide/show UI entry points when module is OFF.

Implemented public glossary endpoints:

GET /glossary/committees

GET /glossary/committees/{committee_id}/settings

GET /glossary/committees/{committee_id}/terms
with public-enabled gating and computed available tags.

Implemented private committee glossary endpoints:

GET /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms

POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms

PUT /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}

POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}/archive

POST /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/terms/{term_id}/delete

GET/PUT /committees/{committee_id}/glossary/settings
with required idempotency on writes and audit logging for write actions/settings updates.

Added backend test coverage in tests/test_glossary_v1.py for module-off strictness, public-disabled behavior, idempotency, role-based moderation rights, filters (query/tags/year), audit presence, and write rate limiting.

  - key, enabled, reason, updated_at, updated_by

   - Idempotency-Key required

ADMIN GLOBAL:

11) POST /admin/purge/run

But : que scan → feedback → action soit instant, même en réseau moyen, et que l’app ne “freeze” jamais en soirée.

Prompt Codex — PR48 (Perf terrain: scanner <1s, prefetch, anti-jank, instrumentation)
Tu es Codex sur TORÈ. Objectif PR48: améliorer la performance terrain (scanner + ops) sans ajouter de nouvelles features produit. Focus: latence perçue <1s sur scan/entry/order, pas de jank UI, et instrumentation légère.

CONTRAINTES
- Ne pas casser les guards: offline/read-only, degraded, runtime gates.
- Pas de refactor massif. Patchs ciblés.
- Pas de dépendance lourde. (OK: flutter/scheduler, dart:developer)
- Pas de logs secrets. Pas de QR brut en logs (hash ok).

OBJECTIFS PRODUIT / DoD
1) Scanner universel:
   - feedback haptique+visuel < 200ms après détection
   - anti double-scan robuste (cooldown + “same payload” suppression)
   - routing/navigation déclenchée sans freeze UI
2) Prefetch:
   - au moment “event actif” et à l’ouverture EventHub, précharger:
     * /events/{id}/context (déjà polling)
     * ticker first page
     * menu list (si module menu ON)
   - ne jamais bloquer le frame; exécuter en microtask / postFrame.
3) Entry staff rush:
   - queue offline: écriture localStorage async + batch flush
   - éviter rebuilds coûteux lors de scans rapides (ListView logs limités, memo)
4) Instrumentation:
   - ajouter un petit PerfTracer:
     * measure “scan_detected -> route_resolved -> screen_pushed”
     * measure “entry_checkin request duration”
   - en DEV seulement: afficher une overlay discrète (option toggle) avec latences moyennes (p50/p95 approximées).
   - en PROD: instrumentation silencieuse (logs off par défaut).

IMPLEMENTATION DETAILLEE

A) Scanner perf
- Dans QrScannerScreen:
  - Dès qu’un code est détecté:
    * déclencher feedback immédiat (haptic + beep + highlight) AVANT parsing réseau
    * stocker `lastScanHash` (sha1 du raw) + timestamp; si même hash < 1200ms => ignore
    * utiliser `SchedulerBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback` pour lancer la navigation, pour éviter jank pendant le frame de camera
  - Découper:
    * detect -> localValidate (expiry local) -> routeDecision -> (optional) backend resolve
  - Ne jamais faire d’appel réseau sur le thread de rendu avant feedback.

B) Prefetch providers
- Créer `lib/core/perf/prefetch.dart`:
  - `prefetchEventEssentials(ref, eventId)`:
     * scheduleMicrotask: invalidate/refresh ticker/menu/context providers si modules ON
     * protéger avec un lock (une fois toutes les 30s) pour éviter spam
- Hook:
  - quand activeEventContextProvider devient “hasValue”: appeler prefetch
  - à l’ouverture EventHubScreen: appeler prefetch (postFrame)
  - sur HomeHub quand event actif: appeler prefetch (postFrame)

C) Entry rush UI optimization
- Dans EntryStaffScreen:
  - limiter le log recent à N=8 items max
  - éviter setState global à chaque scan:
     * utiliser StateNotifier pour log + count
     * UI: ListView.builder avec key stable + const widgets
  - offline queue:
     * écrire sur SharedPreferences via debounce (ex: flush toutes les 500ms ou toutes les 10 entrées)
     * during scanning: ajouter en mémoire + flush async, ne pas await

D) PerfTracer + overlay DEV
- Ajouter `lib/core/perf/perf_tracer.dart`:
  - simple singleton/service (Riverpod provider) qui stocke samples (ring buffer)
  - méthodes:
    * mark(String tag)
    * measure(String name, Duration)
    * timeAsync(String name, Future<T> Function() fn)
  - hashing helper `hashQr(String raw)` pour logs
- Overlay DEV:

Summary
Renforcé le scanner universel pour réduire la latence perçue : feedback immédiat (haptique + son + bannière), suppression robuste des doubles scans via hash + cooldown, validation locale d’expiration avant routage, et déclenchement du routing en post-frame pour éviter le jank pendant le rendu caméra. 

Ajouté une couche de prefetch non bloquante (microtask) avec verrou 30s (PrefetchGate) pour context/ticker/menu(config), puis branché ce prefetch à la fois sur les updates du contexte event, sur l’ouverture de EventHub, et depuis HomeHub quand un event actif existe. 

Optimisé le flux Entry staff rush : logs récents plafonnés à 8, gestion via StateNotifier, rendu via ListView.builder stable, queue offline en mémoire avec persistance asynchrone débouncée (500ms / 10 entrées) sans await durant les scans rapides. 

Ajouté une instrumentation légère PerfTracer + overlay DEV (toggle long-press sur le logo TORÈ au boot, uniquement en env dev), et branché les mesures sur entry_checkin et orderCreate. 

Ajouté les tests unitaires demandés (suppression scan + lock prefetch) et la dépendance crypto pour le hash SHA1 des QR.

Summary
Extended backend event hosting to support user/orga generic host ownership and app-write idempotency storage by adding host_type, host_id, visibility/publication fields, and EventAppWriteIdempotency.

Added sqlite-safe compatibility updates for the new event fields and the app idempotency table so existing sqlite installs can migrate without destructive changes.

Implemented app-oriented event APIs with required Idempotency-Key + audit logging:

POST /events (host user/orga, orga OWNER/MANAGER check, DRAFT default),

POST /events/{event_id}/publish,

GET /events/{event_id}/share (public for PUBLIC),

POST /events/{event_id}/staff_invite_code.

Added orga public event listing endpoint GET /orgas/{org_id}/events to support no-QR discovery flows (Today/Upcoming/Past bucket + runtime state).

Added backend pytest coverage for requested scenarios (host user success, orga permission denial, idempotent create/publish, share links, audit checks).

Added Flutter API client methods for the new backend routes (createEventV1, publishEventV1, getEventShareV1, createStaffInviteCodeV1, listOrgaPublicEvents).
